# Household hh_s5 — 28 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: couple. Rooms: bathroom, bedroom_1, dining, entry, kitchen, living.

Residents:

- **Sam** (resident_1): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.66); punctual (jitter scale 0.59); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.99); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: board_games, meditation, reading_living. Chores they take on: fixing, grocery_run, ironing.
- **Yuki** (resident_2): works outside the home; fairly tidy (tidiness 0.67); punctual (jitter scale 0.77); rarely forgets pocket items; mood strongly affects behaviour (sensitivity 0.84); sleeps in bedroom_1, works at the bedroom_1 desk. Hobbies: gym, plants, snacking. Chores they take on: dusting, grocery_run, ironing, vacuuming.

Pet: a dog, mainly looked after by Sam.

## Day 2 — Wednesday

**Active causes**

- `ep:overslept:resident_1:d2`: Sam overslept: a rushed morning with things dumped at the door, then a normal day (06:30–10:30).
- `ep:restless_evening:resident_1:d2`: Sam can't settle this evening and wanders between rooms with things in hand (21:14–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_1:d2`: Sam is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (10:27–12:27).

Internal states (0–1): Sam energy 0.29, hurriedness 0.39, distraction 0.21; Yuki energy 0.84, hurriedness 0.34, distraction 0.47
